Pushing c7–c5 covers both problems. The c5 pawn defends d4, so Qxd4 would lose the queen to cxd4. The pawn also leaves c7, getting it out of the bishop's line.

I'm working from the diagram alone, so please confirm the move is legal with `get_legal_moves` before submitting.
